give budget
add to the budget (default 1,000,000)
set budget
set the budget to an amount (default 1,000,000)
go into debt
set money to minus an amount (default 100): a safe zone may put planning money into debt, and READY then refuses with a popup until it is paid back
reset debt hint
forget that the one-time "you may go into debt while planning" notice was seen: it shows again the next time planning money drops below $0
reset paint tips
forget that the one-time mine and paratrooper paint tips were seen: each shows again the next time its tool is armed
reset price tip
forget that the one-time rising-price tip was seen: it shows again the next time a second unit of an escalating kind (mortar.cost_increase ...) is placed (escalate.odin)
reset outside tip
forget that the one-time outside-zone tip was seen: it shows again the next time a garrison, sniper, mortar or Turret is placed outside every safe zone (outside_tip_after_place, buildingclass.odin)
max budget
fill the budget
stage size
show what this stage pays and rolls: its land, the civilians it is paid for (capped at the campaign's crowd cap, stage_crowd_cap), the money grant, the carried money, and the outbreak READY will roll (stage_scale.odin)
crowd cap
show this campaign's crowd cap: civilian.max_per_land_km2 x the land inside its last stage (the .cmp's land_km2), at most civilian.max_count, and this stage's natural crowd (stage_crowd_cap, stage_scale.odin)
badge lines
show this stage's seven badge lines: every one but bronze rises with each later campaign stage (badges.<tier>_percent_per_stage, up to <tier>_percent_max -- badges.odin)
badge top
show whether this scenario is big enough for the tiers past diamond: its civilians against badges.secret_tier_civilians, and the top tier it can award (badge_top -- badges.odin)
savings bonus
show the savings bonus (economy.unspent_bonus_percent): the unspent money this campaign stage was entered with and the bonus paid on it, and what the money left now would carry into the next stage
balance
show how the balance levers (balance.ini) meet on this stage: the money, the meters of safe-zone outline it buys at walls.cost per 2 m, and the civilians the bronze line needs
give stock
add N of EVERY item to the battle stock, free (default 5; police get N squads) -- also stocks the planning-only items and decoys, which the loadout never does
recharge abilities
refill every battle ability on the bar now: one that recharges to its full bank of charges (cooldowns start over), one that doesn't to what the loadout gave it at READY
this stage's place on the money-to-SP shift: its crowd, how far it has turned to the commander's game, its grant and its SP (stage_scale.odin)
give sp
grant N extra skill points for the loadout screen (default 10; U during planning to spend them)
unlock next [n]
unlock the next n entries of the unlock order (default 1) with their popups, as n first wins would (unlock.odin, unlocks.ini)
say whether this run is a Sandbox Mode run (FREE PLAY's SANDBOX MODE switch), its live units against sandbox.unit_cap and the rules set; in one, open its panel (sandbox.odin)
with a number 1-8, toggle that perk (in enum order) past its unlock and the slot locks: off if held, else into the first empty slot (or the last) -- the campaign's own one stays in the first slot; with none, free every slot's lock and list the slots (perks.odin)
leaderboard
open the leaderboard screen on this run's board, as the stats popup's VIEW LEADERBOARD does (leaderboard.odin)
daily challenge
leave this run and play the daily challenge, locked or not; with a number N, the one N days from today (daily.odin)
welcome message
show the map's welcome message again, from its campaign file's [welcome] block (mapwelcome.odin)
unlock all
unlock every unit, battle ability, upgrade and perk at once, without popups (unlock.odin), and open every campaign map for this session, as "unlock campaign maps" does (campaignlock.odin)
reset unlocks
lock everything again but the unlock order's [start] entries, and zero the breach-restart count (unlock.odin)
unlock status
show how many unlocks are left and the breach restarts counted toward the next one (unlock.odin)
roster default
reset the planning roster to the seven original placements (roster.odin)
roster equip [n]
equip planning unit n (its place in a PLANNING card's list of options, 1-based) into the first free slot
roster unequip [slot]
empty planning roster slot n (default 7); asks first when that unit has placements, which are refunded
tower upgrades max
the selected tower's own upgrades (every tower's, with none selected) to their top levels, granting the skill points that takes (planning; upgrades.odin)
tower upgrades reset
the selected tower's own upgrades (every tower's, with none selected) back to level 0; their skill points come back (planning)
building units zone rule
toggle safe_zone.building_units_inside_only: garrisons, snipers, mortars and Turrets only into buildings inside a safe zone, until config.ini next reloads (bldg_zone_ok, garrison.odin)
outside zone price
set safe_zone.outside_price_multiplier to the number (x the price of a garrison, sniper, mortar or Turret outside every safe zone; no number = 1), until config.ini next reloads -- the units already placed are repriced without a charge (bldg_price_mult, buildingclass.odin)
tower upgrades hide
toggle upgrades.hide_unit_upgrades: no tower's SP Upgrades (Level Up stays), until config.ini next reloads (upgrades.odin)
sharing ask
raise the one-time game-choice sharing prompt now (usage.odin)
sharing reset
sharing off, its id and queue gone, and the one-time prompt not asked yet (usage.odin)
sharing send
send the queued game-choice records now instead of at the next full batch (usage.odin)
sharing dump
print this battle's game-choice record to the terminal, as sharing would send it (usage.odin)
unlock strikes
skip the battle unlock timers (bomber / fire / gas / heli / APC / bus available at once)
mushroom cloud
put a mushroom cloud up at the cursor (drawing only; reshape it live from [mushroom] in config.ini)
mushroom cloud repeat
toggle a mushroom cloud going up at the cursor again each time the last one is gone, for tuning [mushroom] by eye
clear mushroom clouds
take every mushroom cloud down (and the repeat off)
unlock campaign maps
toggle every campaign map open on the campaign list and the globe for this session, whatever has been beaten, and unlock nothing else (campaignlock.odin)
unlock all dlc
toggle every DLC feature on without owning the DLC (today: the globe's nukes and satellites, 5488850), to test them on a build without Steam
globe satellites
show N satellites (default 3) round the campaign globe whatever campaigns are beaten, to test them (0 = back to one per beaten campaign; they still need the DLC or "unlock all dlc")
plant flag
drop a broken-wall marker flag at the cursor (test the flag rendering)
clear flags
pull every broken-wall marker flag
ui scale
set the UI scale setting (the settings screen's UI scale): a multiple of the display's DPI scale, 0.75..2 (default 1); the window caps what shows
font size
set the Font size setting (the settings screen's Font size): a font file's letters as a multiple of [fonts] line_height, 1..1.3 (default 1); a built-in font ignores it
flag size
set the flags' size setting (the settings screen's Flag size): a multiple of the configured size, 0.25..3 (default 1)
flag brightness
set the flags' brightness setting (the settings screen's Flag brightness): 0..2, 1 = their own fade, 2 = every flag opaque (default 1)
glow brightness
set the zombie glow's brightness setting (the settings screen's Zombie glow brightness): 0..2, 1 = as [glow] draws it, 0 = no glow (default 1)
controls
open the settings screen on its CONTROLS tab: click a control's key to rebind it
reset controls
put every key binding back on its default (the CONTROLS tab's RESET TO DEFAULTS)
colour vision
set the Colour vision palette: 0 off, 1 protanopia, 2 deuteranopia, 3 tritanopia (no number: the next one)
unit size
set the Unit size setting: a multiple of every dot's drawn size, 1..3 (default 1)
toggle reduce flashing
turn the Reduce flashing setting on / off: soft blasts with a ring at their edge, dimmed muzzle flashes
toggle screen shake
turn the Screen shake setting on / off: the camera's jolt when a nuke goes off
shake screen
jolt the camera as a nuke does (nuke.shake_px for nuke.shake_seconds), to try the Screen shake setting
test kill marks
flash N (default 12) kill markers scattered round the cursor, as if the selected gun had just killed there (killmarks.odin; kill_marks.max_per_tick does not apply)
damage wall
knock N HP (default 100) off the wall part nearest the cursor, never below 1 HP -- to test the wall hover readout's WEAKEST POINT and the weak-wall glow, which lights once a part is below walls.weak_glow_below of its wall's full health (repeat it to watch the glow grow)
breach wall
wear the wall part nearest the cursor through, so the next battle tick opens a hole there as if the horde had chewed through it -- the safe zone it belongs to turns its tint and glow red, its worn walls stop glowing, a red circle marks the hole (its first walls.breach_markers_per_zone breaches) and its guns lose their endless ammo (battle only)
panic civilians here
set every civilian within a radius of the cursor (default 100) to full panic: they run from the nearest zombies at their flee speed (half the zombies') -- and the ones inside an unbreached safe zone calm straight back down
count sheltered
count the civilians inside the safe zones, and how many of them are sheltered from panic (their part of the zone not breached) -- civilians walk in through a zone's walls but never out
zone ammo
count the safe-zone regions drawn red because no zone they lie in holds a civilian (EMPTY: no safe zone, no endless ammo), and the guns on endless ammo -- in planning, those READY would leave endless; in battle, those whose region is not breached yet (ammo.odin)
clear replay
forget the battle-replay recording (the deployments that would replay next battle) and this battle's scheduled ones
take back the last change to the planning board (Ctrl+Z)
clear safe zones
remove every safe zone and its walls, refunded (planning only)
zone limits
show the safe zones against their limits and their land price: how many zones the board holds out of safezone_limit (safe_zone.free_zones + EXTRA SAFE ZONE levels), the share of the stage's land they cover together against the land cap (safezone_land_cap: safe_zone.max_land_percent on a small stage down to max_land_percent_large on a big one), and what their ground costs (each safe area's share and price printed to the console) (zoneland.odin)
wall length
show the standalone walls' (K tool) summed straight-line length against this level's cap (walls.max_standalone_length + max_standalone_length_per_level a level after the first); with a number, set the level-1 cap for this session (0 = no cap) (wallline.odin)
zone price
show what the safe zones cost: their outline's straight-line length inside the play area (outside it is free) -- walled or not, each stretch paid once, at walls.cost per 2 m -- their start fees (safe_zone.start_fee, walls.start_fee), and how many of its grid cells hold a wall and how many cross buildings or water (safezone.odin)
move zone corner here
move the safe-zone corner nearest the cursor (any distance) to the ground under the cursor, exactly as dragging it would: the zone is re-laid, its walls refunded / charged, one Ctrl+Z step (planning only)
add zone corner here
add a corner to the safe-zone edge nearest the cursor (any distance), at the point on it nearest the cursor, exactly as a right-click on the edge would: the zone is re-laid, its walls refunded / charged, one Ctrl+Z step (planning only)
delete zone corner
delete the safe-zone corner nearest the cursor (any distance), exactly as a right-click on it would: its two edges join into one, the zone is re-laid, its walls refunded / charged, one Ctrl+Z step (planning only; a zone keeps at least 3 corners)
new outbreak
forget this stage's outbreak seed, so the next READY rolls a new outbreak even on an unchanged plan (normally the same plan meets the same outbreak until the stage is won, and any plan change moves it, outbreak.odin)
coop invite
co-op: open a Steam lobby and Steam's invite dialog -- the globe's INVITE FRIEND button does the same (a COOP and STEAM build)
coop host
co-op: host a session on a direct link (the number is the port; 27015 by default) -- the other player runs "coop join" or starts with --coop-join <address>:<port>
coop join
co-op: join a session hosted on this machine (the number is the port; 27015 by default); another machine: start with --coop-join <address>:<port>
coop leave
co-op: leave the session (the other player is told)
coop ping
co-op: ping the middle of the screen, as an Alt+left click on the map pings (both screens)
coop status
co-op: show the session -- the link, the battle tick, both machines' seals, the round trip, the shared board's version, where the host is
edge spawn
toggle outbreak.edge_spawn until the next config reload: the next READY's outbreak walks in from the map's edges in waves instead of erupting inside it (edgespawn.odin)
edge wave
bring the next edge-spawn wave in on the next tick (a battle READY started with outbreak.edge_spawn on)
repeat outbreak
make the next READY roll the last battle's outbreak again, once, even on a changed plan (normally any plan change moves the outbreak), so a plan change can be tried against it
debug dump
write everything needed to look into this moment to debug_dumps/ beside the exe (also F6): a readable .txt (map, stage, seed, tick, cursor, plan, walls, the units near the cursor, every setting) and a .state file of the whole sim state, which tools/dumpreplay steps on headlessly
crash test
crash the game on purpose (a write through a null pointer), to check the crash log: on Windows it leaves logs/crash-<stamp>.txt and a .dmp minidump beside the exe (crashlog_windows.odin)
gpu lost test
act as if the graphics device were lost (a driver reset), to check what that does: on Windows the planning board is saved, logs/gpu-lost-<stamp>.txt is written, a message box says so and the game closes (gpulost_windows.odin). Elsewhere it does nothing
remove spawn point
delete the outbreak site nearest the cursor for THIS BATTLE only, and every zombie it spawned, wherever it stands (civilians it infected stay). Sites stacked on the very same point go together. The outbreak is rolled at READY, so this works in battle (or on a dev scenario's fixed [spawns] crosshair during planning); the next READY rolls a new outbreak anyway. The number is how far from a site's center the cursor may be (default 300)

# The order the battle abilities are listed in when a BATTLE card on the loadout screen is
# opened (src/battleorder.odin; *Loadout* in docs/gameplay.md). The unlocked ones are listed
# first in this order, then the locked ones in this order.
# One ability per line, by the name the game knows it by: Police (paratroopers), Bomber
# (airstrike), Heli, Stungas (gas bomb), Firebomb, CallArms, APC, Rally, Bus, Missile,
# Medevac, Dozer, Tank, Cure, Nuke, Demolition, SeekDestroy, Cluster (cluster bomb).
# Reorder freely: it changes only the list, never a save. Abilities not listed here follow,
# in the game's own order; each build appends them to the end of this file
# (tools/order-fill.sh), to be moved. Read again each time a card's list is opened.

# Zombies Aren't Real -- the unlock order (src/unlock.odin; *Unlocks* in docs/gameplay.md).
# A new player starts with only what [start] lists. Every scenario won for the first time,
# and every [rules] breach_restarts_per_unlock RETRYs of a battle in which a safe-zone wall
# broke, unlocks the next entry of [order] the player doesn't have yet -- with a popup that
# names it. Reorder, add or remove lines freely: the player's save lists what they have by
# name, so a change here never takes an unlock away, it only changes what comes next.
# One entry per line: a kind, then the name the game knows it by.
#   unit <Tool>       a planning placement. Garrison, Sniper, Mortar, Nest (the Turret),
#                     Mine, C4, Battleship, GasMortar, FireMortar, Railgun,
#                     Howitzer, MLRS, ChemMine, Spikes, Oil, Napalm
#   ability <Item>    a battle deployable bought with SP. Police (paratroopers), Bomber
#                     (airstrike), Heli, Stungas (gas bomb), Firebomb, CallArms, APC, Rally,
#                     Bus, Missile, Medevac, Dozer, Tank, Cure, Nuke, Demolition, SeekDestroy,
#                     Cluster (cluster bomb), AC130 (the AC-130 gunship)
#   upgrade <Upgrade> a tower's own SP upgrade (Garrison_Rate, Sniper_Range, Ship_Blast ...)
# A tower's upgrade waits until its unit is unlocked (Sniper_Range comes after Sniper,
# wherever it is listed). Anything this build leaves out (the playtest's cut units) is
# skipped. Anything listed in neither section still unlocks: after everything listed here,
# in the game's own order (units, then abilities, then upgrades) -- and each build appends
# such entries to the end of this file in that order (tools/order-fill.sh), to be moved.
[rules]
breach_restarts_per_unlock = 2 # RETRYs after a breached wall that earn one unlock
[start]
unit Garrison
ability Police
[order]
unit Mortar
ability Bomber
unit Sniper
upgrade Garrison_Rate
ability Heli
unit Nest
ability Stungas
unit Mine
upgrade Sniper_Range
ability Firebomb
upgrade Mortar_Blast
upgrade Turret_Rate
unit C4
ability CallArms
upgrade Garrison_Magazine
unit GasMortar
upgrade Sniper_Rate
ability APC
unit Battleship
upgrade Turret_Range
unit FireMortar
ability Rally
upgrade Garrison_Reload
unit ChemMine
ability SeekDestroy
ability Demolition
ability Missile
ability Cluster
ability AC130
upgrade Sniper_Magazine
unit Spikes
upgrade Mortar_Rate
ability Bus
unit Howitzer
ability Dozer
upgrade Turret_Magazine
unit Oil
upgrade Garrison_Reach
unit Railgun
upgrade Sniper_Reload
unit Napalm
ability Tank
upgrade Turret_Reload
unit MLRS
upgrade Ship_Reload
upgrade Ship_Blast
ability Cure
ability Medevac
ability Nuke

#version 430
uniform vec4 b3d_params[6];
layout(location = 1) in vec2 hf;
layout(location = 1) out vec2 world;
layout(location = 0) in vec2 pos;
layout(location = 2) in vec4 color0;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 color;
void main()
    float _28 = hf.y * b3d_params[4].w;
    world = pos;
    if ((color0.w < 0.0040000001899898052215576171875) || (_28 > 0.99500000476837158203125))
        gl_Position = vec4(0.0, 0.0, -2.0, 1.0);
        color = vec4(0.0);
